#### Gittinger's Personality Assessment System

**Expressing Original Component**:
- A person can express their original personality component

**Suppression or Repression**:
- A child can suppress or repress their original personality component
- Suppression/repression occurs during the following periods:
  - Compensation period
  - Modification period

**Change in Personality Traits**:
- The intensity of punishment and pressure to change determines the amount of change in a person's mind regarding their original personality

**Personality Assessment System (PAS)**:
- Provides a simplified idea of how PAS works
  - Essential dynamics of a personality can be written in a short code
  - Basic 3 dimensions provide 8 basic types, which can change in 5 different ways
  - Second change can be done on 4 types of modification

#### PAS test explanation: Wechsler Intelligence Test basis, E-I (Internalizer-Externalizer) and R-F (Regulated-Flexible) dimensions, EEG use for personality assessment in children, Monarch program involvement.

**PAS Test Explanation:**
* Based on Wechsler Intelligence Test's digit-span subtest
* Initial ideas from observations of short order cooks and infants
* **E-I Dimension**: Internalizers (I) have good number memory, pressured to be more outgoing; Externalizers (E) prefer doing over thinking, become sloppy drunks after several drinks.
* Predictions for LSD effect based on E-I dimension.
* Block design subtest determines Regulated (R) or Flexible (F) personality: R learn by rote, F must understand before learning; charisma vs ignorance.
* Role Adaptive (A) or Role Uniform (U) person: A defined as Charisma, U ignored.
* CIA use of PAS and Wechsler subtests for assessing children's behavior and personality traits.
* EEG patterns correlate with PAS digit-span test results, used to overcome cultural bias in testing.
* Early research on connection between EEGs and pre-verbal personality assessment: Saunders (1961), Mundy-Castle (1960), Salvatore et al. (1954).
* Children are trained to be obedient for EEG testing, keep eyes open and still.
* Use of hospital equipment like 11-channel Grass EEG machine to chart brain wave patterns.


#### Brain wave testing for personality assessment and Monarch programming using electrodes.

**Brain Wave Testing for Personality Assessment**

**Preparation:**
- Electrodes attached to child's scalp with adhesive tape or needled ends (T3, T4 etc.)
- Child kept still and eyes open, not to move or blink
- Procedure repeated for 45 minutes to an hour

**Assessment:**
- Brain waves charted to determine personality type
- Reveals strengths, weaknesses, mental instability
- Important for knowing how to structure alter system in programming

**Personality Assessment Criteria:**
- Internalizer vs. Externalizer
  * Internalizers: important for deep alters
  * Externalizers: useful for front personalities
- Regulated (R) vs. Role Adaptive (A) personalities
  * R's readily accept authority and limited activity
  * A's may or not be wanted depending on the role requirements

**Brain Wave Patterns:**
- IFU children: often autistic
- IRU: schizophrenic children
- IR or IF: fantasy prone

**Usage in Programming:**
- Knowing child's personality helps fine tune programming
- Children good at math have scripts steered towards math-focused roles
- Artistically gifted children may use art work for programming.

#### Mind control through layered programming using false memories and emotions

**Alice In Wonderland Programming Techniques**

**Planting Thoughts**:
- A thought is planted in the victim's mind
- Linked to a false memory created via hypnosis, movies, or other methods
- Linked to a command put in by hypnosis etc.
- All linked together by a single emotion

**Layered Programming**:
- Each alter (personality) is used as a component of the system
- No single alter is the whole, just a cog in the machine

**Alice In Wonderland Trance Induction**:
- Alters can be sent into deep trances by internal programmers and other alters
- Process involves going down the tree, through the mirror, or growing tall (like Alice In Wonderland)
- The alter drinks "love potion #9" which shrinks them to a dot, then goes through the keyhole
- As they ascend back up in trance, they stretch and enlarge
- Backup amnesia is programmed to prevent the alter from remembering the process

**Trance Induction Imagery**:
- Stories like Wizard of Oz (going into the poppies), Star Wars (time warp, riding the light), or Star Trek (being beamed, replicators) are used as the story line for trance induction.
